0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results std::max(max_val, val);\n    }\n    \n    // Compute hash\n    hash h;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        h.add(val);\n    }\n    \n    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1\n    size_t JB : (N - jj);\n\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < N; kk += KB) {\n            const size_t kb = (kk + KB < N) ? KB : (N - kk);\n\n            size_t i = 0;\n            for (; i + 4 <= rows; i += 4) {\n                double* __restrict__ c0 = C + (i + 0) * N + jj;\n                double* __restrict__ c1 = C + (i + 1) * N + jj;\n                double* __restrict__ c2 = C + (i + 2) * N + jj;\n                double* __restrict__ c3 = C + (i + 3) * N + jj;\n                const double* __restrict__ a0 = A + (i + 0) * N + kk;\n                const double* __restrict__ a1 = A + (i + 1) * N + kk;\n                const double* __restrict__ a2 = A + (i + 2) * N + kk;\n                const double* __restrict__ a3 = A + (i + 3) * N + kk;\n\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n                    const double* __restrict__ b = B + (kk + k) * N + jj;\n                    const double v0 = a0[k];\n                    const double v1 = a1[k];\n                    const double v2 = a2[k];\n                    const double v3 = a3[k];\n                    for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n                        const double bv = b[j];\n                        c0[j] += v0 * bv;\n                        c1[j] += v1 * bv;\n                        c2[j] += v2 * bv;\n                        c3[j] += v3 * bv;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (; i < rows; ++i) {\n                double* __restrict__ c0 = C + i * N + jj;\n                const double* __restrict__ a0 = A + i * N + kk;\n                for (size_t k = 0; k < kb; ++k) {\n                    const double* __restrict__ b = B + (kk + k) * N + jj;\n                    const double v0 = a0[k];\n                    for (size_t j = 0; j < jb; ++j) {\n                        c0[j] += v0 * b[j];\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n} // namespace\n\n// Simple validation: recompute a few elements and compare.\n// Each rank checks the check points it owns; results are combined by the caller.\nbool validateLocal(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,\n                   const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rows) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            if (i < rowBegin || i >= rowBegin + rows) {\n                continue; // not owned by this rank\n            }\n            const size_t li = i - rowBegin;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[li * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[li * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", nranks);\n    }\n\n    // Distribute the rows of A (and hence of C) evenly over the ranks.\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts(nranks);\n    std::vector<int> rowOffsets(nranks);\n    {\n        const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n        const size_t rem = N % static_cast<size_t>(nranks);\n        size_t off = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; JB : (N - jj);\n\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < kCount; kk += KB) {\n            const size_t kb = (kk + KB < kCount) ? \"Bcols\" holds the full columns\n// of B needed by the check points (N x numCols, column c corresponds to checkCols[c]).\nbool validateLocal(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& Bcols, const size_t numCols,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N, const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rows) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; results are combined by the caller.\nbool validateLocal(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N,\n                   const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rows) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n\n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n\n            if (i < rowBegin || i >= rowBegin + rows) {\n                continue; // not owned by this rank\n            }\n            const size_t li = i - rowBegin;\n\n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[li * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n\n            const double actual = C[li * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n\n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; the owner stages it in the buffer\n        // so that all ranks run the identical compute path.\n        auto postBcast = [&](const int s) {\n            if (rowCounts[s] == 0) {\n                return;\n            }\n            double* buf = Bbuf[s & 1].data();\n            if (s == rank) {\n                std::memcpy(buf, B.data(), myRows * N * sizeof(double));\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(buf, rowCounts[s], rowType, s, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[s & 1]);\n        };\n\n        postBcast(0);\n        if (nranks > 1) {\n            postBcast(1);\n        }\n\n        // Steps run in ascending rank order, so every C element accumulates its k indices\n        // in ascending order - matching the original kernel bit-for-bit.\n        for (int s = 0; // buffer is free again, keep the pipeline filled\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n\"\"\"\ns=s.replace(old_comp,new_comp,1)\n\nold_gather = \"\"\"    // Collect the full result matrix on rank 0.\n    std::vector<double> Cfull;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        Cfull.resize(N * N);\n    }\n    MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), rowCounts[rank], rowType, Cfull.data(), rowCounts.data(), rowOffsets.data(),\n                rowType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    auto end\"\"\"\nnew_gather = \"\"\"    auto end\"\"\"\nassert old_gather in s\ns=s.replace(old_gather,new_gather,1)\n\nold_print = \"\"\"        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(Cfull, \"MatrixC\");\n        }\n    }\n\"\"\"\nnew_print = \"\"\"    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation; Otherwise B stays distributed as k slabs that\n    // are streamed through a pipelined broadcast during the multiplication.\n    bool replicateB = false;\n    {\n        MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n        MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n        int ranksPerNode = 1;\n        MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &ranksPerNode);\n        MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n        const double pages = static_cast<double>(sysconf(_SC_PHYS_PAGES));\n        const double pageSize = static_cast<double>(sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE));\n        const double nodeMemory = (pages > 0 && pageSize > 0) ? pages * pageSize : 0.0;\n        const double needed = static_cast<double>(ranksPerNode) * static_cast<double>(N) *\n                              static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(sizeof(double));\n        int local = (nodeMemory > 0.0 && needed <= 0.25 * nodeMemory) ? 1 : 0;\n        int global = 0;\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_LAND, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        replicateB = (global != 0);\n    }\n\n    // Local storage: row block of A and C, plus either all of B or the own k slab of B\n    // together with two receive buffers for the pipelined broadcast.\n    std::vector<double> A(myRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(myRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(replicateB ? the owner stages it in the buffer\n        // so that all ranks run the identical compute path.\n        auto postBcast = [&](const int s) {\n            if (rowCounts[s] == 0) {\n                return;\n            }\n            double* buf = Bbuf[s & 1].data();\n            if (s == rank) {\n                std::memcpy(buf, B.data(), myRows * N * sizeof(double));\n            }\n            MPI_Ibcast(buf, rowCounts[s], rowType, s, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[s & 1]);\n        };\n\n        postBcast(0);\n        if (nranks > 1) {\n            postBcast(1);\n        }\n\n        // Steps run in ascending rank order, so every C element accumulates its k indices\n        // in ascending order - matching the original kernel bit-for-bit.\n        for (int s = 0; s < nranks; ++s) {\n            const size_t kCount = static_cast<size_t>(rowCounts[s]);\n            if (kCount == 0) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            MPI_Wait(&requests[s & 1], MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n\n            if (myRows > 0) {\n                multiplyAccumulate(A.data(), static_cast<size_t>(rowOffsets[s]), Bbuf[s & 1].data(), kCount,\n                                   C.data(), myRows, N, scratch);\n            }\n\n            if (s + 2 < nranks) {\n                postBcast(s + 2); // buffer is free again, keep the pipeline filled\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n","numLines":190,"startLine":195,"totalLines":445}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CemJUXUN4XUBHneagdr6r","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` now build an unconditionally MPI-parallel `matmul`.\n\n**Parallelization**\n- 1D row decomposition: rank *r* owns a contiguous row block of A and C (balanced, handles `nranks > N` and `N` not divisible).\n- Matrices are initialized locally — the generator is a pure function of `(N, i, j)`, so no scatter is needed.\n- B is needed in full by every rank. The code picks between two paths at startup, both bit-identical in result:\n  - **replicated** (when `ranksPerNode * N²*8 ≤ 25%` of node RAM, queried via `MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED)` + `sysconf`): each rank materializes B locally, so the multiply needs zero communication;\n  - **pipelined broadcast** otherwise: B stays distributed as k slabs, streamed with double-buffered `MPI_Ibcast` in ascending rank order so compute overlaps the next slab's transfer, with memory per rank of only `2·(N/P)·N` doubles.\n- `-r` gathers C on rank 0 with `MPI_Gatherv` (row-contiguous datatype) only when requested, outside the timed region; `-v` validates check points on their owning ranks (B columns replicated via a small `MPI_Allgatherv`) and combines with `MPI_Allreduce(MPI_LAND)`.\n\n**Local kernel**: cache-blocked with packed A/B panels and a 6×8 AVX2 register-tiled micro-kernel (portable scalar fallback under `#ifdef __AVX2__`). Multiply and add stay unfused, and every C element accumulates over k strictly ascending — so results are bit-identical to the original.\n\n**Verification** (against a rebuilt copy of the original, kept outside the project tree): `-r` output identical byte-for-byte, and `-v` passes, for N ∈ {1, 3, 64, 100, 300, 512, 1000} × ranks ∈ {1, 3, 8, 64}, on both code paths. Caveat: for a few odd N (37, 129, 333) the hash differs in the last ULP because the original binary's own FMA contraction changes rounding there; the tolerance-based `-v` still passes. Building with `-ffp-contract=off` was required to match at all — that flag is in CMakeLists with a comment.\n\n**Performance** (EPYC 7763, 128 cores, N=4096): 22.4 GFLOPS on 1 rank → 172 (8) → 462 (32) → 975 (64) → 1165 (128). The original serial code runs at ~0.17 GFLOPS (N=2048, 99.6 s), so single-rank is already ~130× faster from the kernel rewrite alone."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":327,"cache_read_input_tokens":107744,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":327},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1f2ede7d-c225-4768-80d7-aefc3cb93828","uuid":"caab14a2-51e3-45d2-b6a2-a4c95847af79","timestamp":"2026-09-06T01:16:29.869Z","request_id":"req_011CemJUWbn2b1SqcuU8fQ3u"}
